# Wardogs: Weapon Shop, Attachments and Components

Structured tables for the weapon bundles under [`Weapons/Attachments`](https://github.com/Kiyogitpy/wardogs-data-docs/tree/main/data/Wardogs/Plugins/GameFeatures/Weapons/Attachments), [`Weapons/Components`](https://github.com/Kiyogitpy/wardogs-data-docs/tree/main/data/Wardogs/Plugins/GameFeatures/Weapons/Components) and [`Weapons/Platforms`](https://github.com/Kiyogitpy/wardogs-data-docs/tree/main/data/Wardogs/Plugins/GameFeatures/Weapons/Platforms). The tables are generated by `_tools/weapon_vendor_tables.py` (`--write`). They sit next to the folder summary in [Weapons (feature)](Wardogs_GameFeatures_Weapons_v2.md) section 6, and the final attribute values in [Weapon Attributes](../content/Wardogs_WeaponAttributes_v1.md). Armor, helmets, backpacks, medical items, throwables, explosives, tools and vehicles are in [Item Shop](Wardogs_ItemShop_v1.md); ammo is in [Items](../content/Wardogs_Items_v2.md).

## Sources and labels

| Label | Source |
|---|---|
| **Data** | `ID_*` item definitions (`BHInventoryItemDefinition`): item id, attachment type tag, sub-slot containers, default items. |
| **Data** | `DT_EntityData_*` (`BHEntityData_UI`): display name, subtitle, shop text. `DT_VendorData_*` and `DT_WeaponVendor*` (`WDVendorData`): price, unlock level, required items, buy limit, variations. |
| **Derived** | Fit groups and caliber. A weapon accepts an item when the item passes the sub-slot filter of one of the weapon's containers, or of a container of an attachment that fits (3 levels, same rule as [Weapon Attributes](../content/Wardogs_WeaponAttributes_v1.md)). Caliber is the allow-list of the weapon's `Chamber` container. |
| **Native** | none used. The slot filter itself is documented in the Weapon Attributes page (RVA `0x67A2B40`). |

The vendor rows hold no currency field. "Price" is the `Price` of the first variation with `bShowInVendor = true`, in the unit the vendor UI uses (not decoded here). "Variations" is how many variations the row has; the others are hidden price points and are not decoded. "Req. items" is `UnlockRequiredItems` of the shown variation.

## 1. Weapon platforms

Launcher rows (9K333 Verba, MAAWS, MGL-40, RPG-7): the rocket and grenade damage, radii, falloff and damage to infantry and vehicles are in [Explosives](Wardogs_Explosives_v1.md).

One row per weapon definition with a stats asset. Price, level and category come from the weapon's vendor row. Default attachments are the items the weapon's containers start with (`DefaultContainerItems`, else `DefaultItems`), including sub-slots of those items.

## 3. Components

Folders `Barrels`, `Default Sights` (`IRNS_*`), `Dust Covers`, `Handguards`, `Pistol Grips`, `Rails`, `Stocks`, `Triggers`. Components have no vendor or entity row; the name is the `DisplayName` of the item definition. "Provides sub-slots" lists the containers the component adds to a weapon (slot, filter method, count of allowed items plus allowed context tags). A `*` after the slot means the slot comes from a context tag because the item has no attachment type tag.

## 5. File index

Every exported file in the three families by folder, anim sets omitted. A folder that lists only its own id (for example [`MUZL_001`](https://github.com/Kiyogitpy/wardogs-data-docs/blob/main/data/Wardogs/Plugins/GameFeatures/Weapons/Attachments/Muzzle/MUZL_001/Content/MUZL_001.json)) ships only the `GameFeatureData` shell: no `Content/Data` folder, so no item definition, vendor row or entity row was exported for it.

## Open

- Currency and the unit of `Price` are not traced. No vendor row names a currency.
- Hidden vendor variations (for example the extra magazine price points) are not decoded.
- Fit is derived from the filters, not from a game table. The shipped client may add runtime rules not seen in the data.
- Folders with only a `GameFeatureData` shell have no data in this export; whether their items live elsewhere is not traced.
